# Every resource Bashiri Smith and Melvin Vivas and Melvin Vivas mention

1232 resources. Links: 1162 verified, 46 open only in a browser, 6 sent only by DM, 3 broken, 15 without a link. Each entry links the reel or post it came from. Most-mentioned first within each group.

## Guides only sent by DM (6)

## Research papers (12)

- **[AutoDesign (automated e-commerce poster design)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13560)** · paper · arxiv.org · free  
  A paper on improving an agent's harness through an inner fix loop and an outer meta-harness learning loop, applied to long-horizon design.  
  Mentioned in: Paperscrolling: Browse Trending AI Research Papers Like a Social Feed (alphaXiv) (Melvin Vivas on [X](https://x.com/melvindvivas/status/2090100050982777186) · [notes](notes/2026-08-19-paperscrolling-browse-trending-ai-research-papers-like-a.md)), AutoDesign: Improving the Harness Around the Model with a Meta-Harness Loop (Melvin Vivas on [X](https://x.com/melvindvivas/status/2089692130348601367) · [notes](notes/2026-08-18-autodesign-improving-the-harness-around-the-model-with-a.md))
- **[arXiv paper: MCP vs. CLI agents benchmark (7 agents, 5 models)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08654)** · paper · arxiv.org · free  
  Academic study comparing agents with and without MCP on a software task, measuring reliability and cost.  
  Mentioned in: Agent Harness Efficiency: Scaffolding Beats MCP vs. CLI (Melvin Vivas on [X](https://x.com/melvindvivas/status/2090772830120222890) · [notes](notes/2026-08-21-agent-harness-efficiency-scaffolding-beats-mcp-vs-cli.md))
- **[Attention Is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/1706.03762)** · paper · arxiv.org · free  
  The 2017 Google paper that introduced the Transformer and scaled dot-product / multi-head attention.

- **[Mind Viruses: Self-Propagating Ideas in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10218)** · paper · arxiv.org · free

- **[Self-Flow (research paper)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06507)** · paper · arxiv.org · free
